PROSPECTS                                                                     前景
We expect the global stock markets to be full of challenges in the            我們預計未來數月全球股市將面臨諸多挑戰，尤
coming months, especially the effects from the timing and extent of           其是美國進一步降息的時機與幅度，以及美國總
further interest rate cuts in U.S. and effects from the ongoing tariff        統特朗普於二零二五年四月初宣布大規模關稅措
and trade disputes after the announcement of sweeping tariffs in              施後持續的關稅與貿易爭端，這引發了自2019

Pursuant to code provisions C.3.3 in respect of the service term of            根據該守則之守則條文第 C.3.3 條，就董事服務
directors, none of the existing Directors (including independent non-          任期而言，本公司現時之董事（包括獨立非執行
executive Directors) of the Company is appointed for a specific term.          董事）並無特定任期，此偏離該守則之守則條文
This constitutes a deviation from code provision C.3.3 of the Code.            第 C.3.3 條。然而，本公司全體董事須遵守本公
However, all the Directors of the Company are subject to the retirement        司組織章程細則第168 條之退任條文。因此，本
provisions under article 168 of the Articles of Association of the             公司認為已採取足夠措施，以確保本公司之企業
Company. As such, the Company considers that sufficient measures               管治常規不遜於該守則所載者。
have been taken to ensure that the Company’s corporate governance
practices are no less exacting than those in the Code.

      as management commitment to the long term growth. However,               長之企業。然而，本公司亦具彈性考慮投
      the Company is flexible in considering investment in companies           資於董事會及投資管理人認為情況特殊
      or other entities which are considered by the Board and the              或正在逐漸復甦之公司或其他企業；
      Investment Manager as being special or in recovery situations;

Investment restrictions                                                   投資限制
Under the Articles and the Listing Rules relating to the listing of       根據章程細則及有關投資公司上市之上市規則，
investment companies, certain restrictions on investment are imposed      本公司投資須受到若干限制。為符合該等限制，
on the Company. In part to meet such restrictions, the Board has          董事會已決議，本公司不可：
resolved that the Company may not:


1.    either itself or through its wholly-owned subsidiaries or in        1.   自行或透過其全資附屬公司或聯同任何
      conjunction with any connected person take legal, or effective,          關連人士取得其投資之合法或有效管理
      management control of underlying investment and in no event,             控制權，且在任何情況下，本公司概不會
      will the Company itself or through its wholly-owned subsidiaries         自行或透過其全資附屬公司擁有或控制
      own or control more than 30%, (or such lower percentage as               任何一間公司或機構超過30%（或收購守
      may from time to time be specified in the Takeover Code as               則不時規定可引發強制性全面收購之較
      being the level for triggering a mandatory general offer) of the         低百分比）之投票權；
      voting rights in any one company or body;


2.    invest in any company or entity other than wholly-owned             2.   於任何公司或其他實體（本公司之全資附
      subsidiaries of the Company if such investment will result in            屬公司除外）作出投資，倘若該等投資超
      more than 20% of the net asset value being invested in such              逾本公司於投資當日之資產淨值20%；
      company or entity as at the date the investment is made;

meeting of the Company at least once every three years and eligible for       格膺選連任。
re-election.


Function                                                                      職能
The Board of Directors, headed by the Chairman, is responsible for            董事會由主席領導，負責根據規管董事會會議之

in accordance with the rules governing the meeting of the Board               股息，以及監督管理層。
of Directors, the Managing Director’s working guides, Articles of

of the Company’s operations. These executive Directors conduct                與本公司及其附屬公司及聯營公司之高級管理層

subsidiaries and associated companies, at which operational issues
and financial performance are evaluated.

and internal controls are essential, and the Board of Directors plays a key   控為基本所需，而董事會在實行及監控內部財務
role in the implementation and monitoring of internal financial controls.     監控方面扮演重要角色。


The Board of Directors has established procedure to enable Directors,         董事會已訂有程序，讓董事按合理要求，可在適

     Chairman and Managing Director                                               主席及董事總經理
     The roles of the Chairman and the Managing Director are segregated           主席及董事總經理之角色應分開，應由彼此間並
     and assumed by two separate individuals who have no relationship with        無關係之兩名獨立人士擔任，以達到平衡權力及
     each other to strike a balance of power and authority so that the job        職權，致使工作職責不會集中於任何一人。
     responsibilities are not concentrated on any one individual.


     The Chairman of the Board is responsible for the leadership and              董事會主席負責領導及有效管理董事會。主席亦
     effective running of the Board. The Chairman is also responsible for         負責釐定每次董事會會議之議程，並考慮將其他
     deciding the agenda of each Board meeting, taking into account, where        董事提出之事務（如適當）包含於議程內。
     appropriate, matters proposed by other Directors for inclusion in the
     agenda.


     The Managing Director is delegated with the authorities to manage the        董事總經理獲授予職權以有效方式管理本集團業
     Group’s business in all aspects effectively, implement major strategies,     務之各方面事務、執行重要策略、作出日常決定
     make day-to-day decision and coordinate overall business operation.          及協調整體業務運作。

     opportunities.

     internal and external reporting.                                              關性及可靠性。

     control systems of the Company through the Audit Committee.                   系統之成效。

     of corporate assets and abuse in related party transaction.                   產及濫用關聯方交易。

     other stakeholders, and compliance with all laws and ethics.                  及遵守所有法例及操守規定。

members’ selection process, including but not limited to gender, age,      別、年齡、文化及教育背景、種族、專業經驗、
cultural and educational background, ethnicity, professional experience,   技能、知識及資歷等。董事會所有委任均以用人

based on merits, and have paid due regard for the benefits of diversity    事會成員多元化的裨益。
on the Board in selecting candidates.

BACKGROUND                                                                      背景
UBA Investments Limited (the “Company”) is an investment company                本公司為一間投資公司，而本公司之主要業務為
and principally engages in investment in a diversified portfolio of             投資於上市及非上市公司之多元化投資組合。作
listed and unlisted companies. As an investment company, the                    為投資公司，本公司未直接參與旗下子公司或附
Company does not directly participate in the production and operation           屬公司的生產及營運。然而，本公司於積極尋求
of its subsidiaries. While actively seeking to optimise its investment          優化投資組合，提升股東回報的同時，仍然恪守
portfolio and maximise Shareholders return, the Company lives up to             企業社會責任，平衡股東、社區等各權益人的利
its corporate social responsibility and strikes a balance between the           益，致力取得企業可持續發展。
interests of stakeholders such as the shareholders and the community,
with the ultimate goal of achieving a sustainable corporation.

            related risks as one of the ESG issues. The Group will also                       亦將在作出投資決策前考慮氣候相關問

            Supply Chain Management                                                 供應鏈管理
            As the Group operating in the investment field, which being             由於本集團於投資領域經營業務，以辦
            office based, it does not rely on any major suppliers for its           公室作基地，通常其業務營運無須依賴
            business operation in general. The Group mainly purchases               任何主要供應商。本集團主要根據其聲

            according to their reputation, the quality of goods and services        供應商採購辦公室設備及辦公室用品。
            provided. The Group will select on a competitive basis and take         此外，本集團將在競爭基礎上並考慮其

                                                                                              （「二零二零年修訂
     Amendments”) and Amendments to HKAS 1 Non-current                              本」）及香港會計準則第1號（修訂本）附帶契
     Liabilities with Covenants (the “2022 Amendments”)                             諾的非流動負債（「二零二二年修訂本」）的
                                                                                    影響
     The Group has applied the amendments for the first time in                     本集團已於本年度首次應用該等修訂本。二
     the current year. The 2020 Amendments provide clarification                    零二零年修訂本對將負債分類為流動或非
     and additional guidance on the assessment of rights to defer                   流動負債的報告之日起至少十二個月的延
     settlement for at least twelve months from reporting date for                  期結算權的評估提供了澄清和補充指導，
     classification of liabilities as current or non-current, which:                其中：


     •    specify that the classification of liabilities as current or non-         •   指明流動或非流動負債的分類應基於
          current should be based on rights that are in existence at the                報告期末存在的權利。具體而言，該
          end of the reporting period. Specifically, the classification                 分類不應受管理層於12個月內結算負
          should not be affected by management intentions or                            債的意圖或預期所影響。
          expectations to settle the liability within 12 months.


     •    clarify that the settlement of a liability can be a transfer              •   澄清負債的清償可以是向交易對手轉
          of cash, goods or services, or the entity’s own equity                        讓現金、商品或服務，或實體自身的
          instruments to the counterparty. If a liability has terms that                權益工具。如果負債的條款可以根據
          could, at the option of the counterparty, result in its settlement            交易對手的選擇，通過轉讓實體自身
          by the transfer of the entity’s own equity instruments, these                 的權益工具來結算，只有當實體將選
          terms do not affect its classification as current or non-current              擇權單獨確認為應用香港會計準則第
          only if the entity recognises the option separately as an                     32號金融工具：呈報 的權益工具時，
          equity instrument applying HKAS 32 Financial Instruments:                     這些條款才不影響其作為流動負債或
          Presentation.                                                                 非流動負債的分類。

     NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS                                  綜合財務報表附註

     2.   APPLICATION OF NEW AND AMENDMENTS TO HKFRS                                 2.   應用新訂及經修訂香港財務報告準則會
          ACCOUNTING STANDARDS (continued)                                                計準則（續）
          Amendments to HKFRS Accounting Standards that are                               於本年度強制生效的經修訂香港財務報告
          mandatorily effective for the current year (continued)                          準則會計準則（續）
          Impacts on application of Amendments to HKAS 1 Classification                   應 用 香 港 會 計 準 則 第1號（修 訂 本）將 負 債
          of Liabilities as Current or Non-current and related                            分類為流動或非流動及對香港詮釋第5號的
          amendments to Hong Kong Interpretation 5 (2020) (the “2020                      相關修訂（二零二零年）
                                                                                                    （「二零二零年修訂
          Amendments”) and Amendments to HKAS 1 Non-current                               本」）及香港會計準則第1號（修訂本）附帶契
          Liabilities with Covenants (the “2022 Amendments”) (continued)                  諾的非流動負債（「二零二二年修訂本」）的
                                                                                          影響（續）
          For rights to defer settlement for at least twelve months from                  對於清償自報告日期起遞延至少十二個月
          reporting date which are conditional on the compliance with                     的權利（以遵守契諾為條件），二零二二年
          covenants, the 2022 Amendments specifically clarify that only                   修訂本特別澄清，實體須於報告期末或之
          covenants that an entity is required to comply with on or before                前遵守的契諾方會影響實體將清償負債於
          the end of the reporting period affect the entity’s rights to defer             報告日期後遞延最少十二個月的權利，即
          settlement of a liability for at least twelve months after the reporting        使契諾的遵守情況僅於報告日期後評估。
          date, even if compliance with the covenant is assessed only                     二零二二年修訂本亦訂明，實體於報告日
          after the reporting date. The 2022 Amendments also specify that                 期後必須遵守的契諾（即未來契諾）不會影
          covenants with which an entity must comply after the reporting                  響負債於報告日期分類為流動或非流動。
          date (i.e. future covenants) do not affect the classification of a              然而，倘實體延遲清償負債的權利受限於
          liability as current or non-current at the reporting date. However,             實體於報告期後十二個月內遵守契諾，則
          if the entity’s rights to defer settlement of a liability is subject to         實體須披露資料以使財務報表的使用者可
          the entity complying with covenants within twelve months after                  了解該等負債於報告期後十二個月內要償
          the reporting period, an entity discloses information that enables              還 的 風 險。該 等 資 料 將 包 括 契 諾、相 關 負
          users of financial statements to understand the risk of the liabilities         債的賬面值以及表明實體可能難以遵守契
          becoming repayable within twelve months after the reporting                     諾的事實及情況（如有）。
          period. This would include information about the covenants, the
          carrying amount of related liabilities and facts and circumstances,
          if any, that indicate that the entity may have difficulties complying
          with the covenants.


          In accordance with the transition provision, the Group has applied              根據過渡條文，本集團已將新會計政策追
          the new accounting policy to the classification of liability as current         溯應用於將負債分類為流動或非流動。於
          or non-current retrospectively. The application of the amendments               本年度應用該等修訂本對綜合財務報表並
          in the current year had no material impact on the consolidated                  無重大影響。
          financial statements.

          The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets                    遞延稅項負債及資產的計量反映於報
          reflects the tax consequences that would follow from the                  告期末本集團預計收回或清付其資產
          manner in which the Group expects, at the end of the                      及負債賬面值的方式而引致的稅務後
          reporting period, to recover or settle the carrying amount of             果。
          its assets and liabilities.


          Deferred tax assets and liabilities are offset when there is              遞延稅項資產及負債於可依法以即期
          a legally enforceable rights to set off current tax assets                稅項資產與即期稅項負債抵銷時，以
          against current tax liabilities and when they relate to income            及於其與同一稅務機關所徵收的同一
          taxes levied to the same taxable entity by the same taxation              課稅實體所得稅有關時予以抵銷。
          authority.


          Current and deferred tax are recognised in profit or loss,                即期及遞延稅項於損益內確認，除非
          except when they relate to items that are recognised in other             其與於其他全面收益內確認或直接
          comprehensive income or directly in equity, in which case,                於權益內確認之項目相關，於此情況
          the current and deferred tax are also recognised in other                 下，即期及遞延稅項亦分別於其他全
          comprehensive income or directly in equity respectively.                  面收益內確認或直接於權益內確認。
          Where current tax or deferred tax arises from the initial                 就因對業務合併進行初步會計處理而
          accounting for a business combination, the tax effect is                  產生的即期稅項或遞延稅項而言，稅
          included in the accounting for the business combination.                  務影響乃計入業務合併的會計處理內。


          In assessing any uncertainty over income tax treatments,                  於評估所得稅處理方法之任何不確定
          the Group considers whether it is probable that the relevant              性時，本集團考慮相關稅務機構是否
          tax authority will accept the uncertain tax treatment used,               可能接受獨立組別實體於其所得稅備
          or proposed to be use by individual group entities in their               案內所使用或擬使用之不確定稅項處
          income tax filings. If it is probable, the current and deferred           理方法。若屬可能，即期及遞延稅項
          taxes are determined consistently with the tax treatment in               會按與所得稅備案一致之稅項處理方
          the income tax filings. If it is not probable that the relevant           法釐定。若相關稅務機構不可能接受
          taxation authority will accept an uncertain tax treatment, the            不確定稅項處理方法，各不確定性之
          effect of each uncertainty is reflected by using either the most          影響會使用最近似金額或預期價值反
          likely amount or the expected value.                                      映。

                     of cash; and


               (b)   cash equivalents, which comprises short-term (generally                  (b)   現金等值項目，包括可隨時轉換
                     with an original maturity of three months or less), highly                     為已知金額現金且價值變化風險

                     rather than for investment or other purposes.                                  目的。

               For the purposes of the consolidated statement of cash                         就綜合現金流動表而言，現金及現金
               flows, cash and cash equivalents consist of cash and                           等值項目包括上述定義的現金及現金

               bank overdrafts which are repayable on demand and form                         還銀行透支，構成本集團現金管理的
               an integral part of the Group’s cash management. Such                          一部分。有關透支在綜合財務狀況表

               consolidated statement of financial position.

               the time frame established by regulation or convention in the
               market place.

               at fair value. Transaction costs that are directly attributable                量。因收購或發行財務資產及財務負
               to the acquisition or issue of financial assets and financial                  債（按公平值於損益賬列賬之財務資產
               liabilities (other than financial assets or financial liabilities at           或財務負債除外）而直接產生之交易
               FVTPL) are added to or deducted from the fair value of the                     成本於首次確認時計入財務資產或財
               financial assets or financial liabilities, as appropriate, on initial          務負債（如適用）之公平值或自其公平

          The effective interest method is a method of calculating                   實際利率法乃計算財務資產或財務負
          the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability             債的攤銷成本及於相關期間攤分利息

          the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate               在財務資產或財務負債的預計年期或
          that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and                  較短期間（如適用）內，將估計未來現
          payments (including all fees and points paid or received that              金收款及付款（包括構成實際利率組
          form an integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction          成部份的所付或所收到的所有費用及

          life of the financial asset or financial liability, or, where              確貼現至於初步確認時的賬面淨值所
          appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount on               使用的利率。
          initial recognition.

                established;


          (b)   it is probable that the economic benefits associated with            (b)   與股息相關的經濟利益很可能流
                the dividend will flow to the entity; and                                  入實體；及


          (c)   the amount of the dividend can be measured reliably.                 (c)   股息金額能夠可靠計量。

          •     the contractual terms give rise on specified dates to                •     合約條款於特定日期產生完全為
                cash flows that are solely payments of principal and                       支付本金及未償還本金的利息的
                interest on the principal amount outstanding.                              現金流。

                reporting date as well as the forecast of future conditions.             況的評估及未來狀況預測而作出調整。


                The Group measures the loss allowance equal to 12m                       本集團計量等於12個月預期信貸虧損
                ECL, unless when there has been a significant increase in                的虧損撥備，除非自首次確認起信貸

     3.2 Material accounting policy information (continued)                        3.2 重大會計政策資料（續）

          (iii) Credit-impaired financial assets                                     (iii) 已信貸減值的財務資產
                A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more                   當發生一項或多項違約事件，對
                events of default that have a detrimental impact on the                 財務資產的估計未來現金流量具
                estimated future cash flows of that financial asset have                有負面影響時，即財務資產已信
                occurred. Evidence that a financial asset is credit-                    貸減值。財務資產已信貸減值的
                impaired includes observable data about the following                   證據包括有關以下事件的可觀察
                events:                                                                 證據：


                (a)   significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the             (a)   發行人或借款人面臨重大
                      borrower;                                                               財務困難；


                (b)   a breach of contract, such as a default or past due               (b)   違 反 合 約，如 拖 欠 或 逾 期
                      event;                                                                  事件；


                (c)   the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or                    (c)   出於與借款人的財務困難
                      contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s                          有 關 的 經 濟 或 合 約 原 因，
                      financial difficulty, having granted to the borrower                    借款人的貸款人向借款人
                      a concession(s) that the lender(s) would not                            授出貸款人在其他情況下
                      otherwise consider;                                                     不會考慮的寬限；


                (d)   it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter              (d)   借款人很可能破產或進行
                      bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation; or                        其他財務重組；或


                (e)   the disappearance of an active market for that                    (e)   該財務資產之活躍市場因
                      financial asset because of financial difficulties.                      財務困難而消失。


          (iv) Write-off policy                                                      (iv) 撇銷政策
                The Group writes off a financial asset when there is                    當有資料表明對手方處於嚴重財
                information indicating that the counterparty is in severe               務困境，且並無收回的現實可能
                financial difficulty and there is no realistic prospect                 性（例如，當對手方被清盤或已
                of recovery, for example, when the counterparty has                     進入破產程序）時，本集團撇銷
                been placed under liquidation or has entered into                       財務資產。經考慮法律意見（如
                bankruptcy proceedings. Financial assets written off                    適用）後，根據本集團的收回程
                may still be subject to enforcement activities under                    序，已撇銷的財務資產可能仍然
                the Group’s recovery procedures, taking into account                    受到強制執行活動。撇銷構成取
                legal advice where appropriate. A write-off constitutes                 消確認事件。任何後續收回於損
                a derecognition event. Any subsequent recoveries are                    益賬確認。
                recognised in profit or loss.

                (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                                  (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
                      The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability                   預期信貸虧損的計量取決於違約
                      of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the                 概率、違約損失（即如存在違約
                      loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default.                  時的損失程度）及違約風險。對
                      The assessment of the probability of default and loss                     違約概率及違約損失的評估基於
                      given default is based on historical data and forward-                    歷史數據及前瞻性資料。對預期
                      looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects an                        信貸虧損的估計反映所釐定的公
                      unbiased and probability-weighted amount that is                          正概率加權金額（以發生違約的
                      determined with the respective risks of default occurring                 風險作為權重）。
                      as the weights.


                      Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                          一般而言，預期信貸虧損為本集
                      contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in                       團按照合約應收到的所有合約現
                      accordance with the contract and the cash flows that the                  金流量與本集團預計收到的現金
                      Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective                     流量之間的差額，按首次確認時
                      interest rate determined at initial recognition.                          釐定的實際利率貼現。


                      Interest income is calculated based on the gross                          利息收入基於財務資產的總賬面
                      carrying amount of the financial asset unless the                         值計算，除非財務資產已信貸減
                      financial asset is credit-impaired, in which case interest                值，在此情況下，利息收入基於
                      income is calculated based on amortised cost of the                       財務資產的攤銷成本計算。
                      financial asset.


                      The Group recognises an impairment gain or loss in                        本集團通過在虧損撥備賬中調整
                      profit or loss for all financial instruments by adjusting                 賬面值之方式而在損益賬中就所
                      their carrying amount through a loss allowance account.                   有金融工具確認減值收益或虧
                                                                                                損。


                Derecognition of financial assets                                         終止確認財務資產
                The Group derecognises a financial asset only when the                    僅當對資產現金流之合約權利屆滿
                contractual rights to the cash flows from the asset expires.              時，本集團即會終止確認該項財務資
                                                                                          產。


                On derecognition of a financial asset measured at amortised               於終止確認按攤銷成本計量之財務資
                cost, the difference between the asset’s carrying amount                  產時，有關資產之賬面值與已收及應
                and the sum of the consideration received and receivable is               收代價總和之差額，會於損益賬中確
                recognised in profit or loss.                                             認。

4.   CRITICAL ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENTS AND KEY SOURCES                          4.   關鍵會計判斷及估計不確定性之主要來
     OF ESTIMATION UNCERTAINTY                                                    源
     In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which                 應用附註3.2所述本集團的會計政策時，董
     are described in Note 3.2, the Directors are required to make                事須對未能從其他來源立即得知其賬面值
     judgements, estimates and assumptions about the carrying                     的資產及負債賬面值作出判斷、估計及假
     amounts of assets and liabilities that are not readily apparent              設。估計及相關假設乃以過往經驗及視為
     from other sources. The estimates and underlying assumptions                 相關的其他因素為依據。實際結果可能異
     are based on historical experience and other factors that are                於該等估計。
     considered to be relevant. Actual results may differ from these
     estimates.


     The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an on-              估計及相關假設會持續檢討。倘會計估計
     going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised in             修訂僅影響該期間，則於修訂期間確認；
     the period in which the estimate is revised if the revision affects          或倘修訂影響當前及未來期間，則於修訂
     only that period, or in the period of the revision and future periods        及未來期間確認。
     if the revision affects both current and future periods.

      5.   CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                5.   資本風險管理
           The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group          本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團旗下實
           will be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the            體能夠持續經營，同時透過債務及股本結
           return to shareholders through the optimisation of the debt and             餘的最優化盡量提高股東回報。本集團之
           equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged              整體策略與往年一樣維持不變。
           from the prior year.


           The capital structure of the Group consists of equity, comprising           本集團之資本架構包括股本（計有已發行股
           issued share capital, share premium and accumulated losses.                 本、股份溢價及累計虧損）。


           The Directors review the capital structure on a semi-annual basis.          董事每半年檢討資本架構。在此項檢討工
           As part of this review, the Directors consider the cost of capital          作中，董事考慮資本成本和每類資本附帶
           and the risks associated with each class of capital. Based on               之風險。本集團將依據董事之意見，透過派
           recommendations of the Directors, the Group will balance its                息及發行新股份以平衡其整體資本架構。
           overall capital structure through the payment of dividends and new
           share issues.

                 amortised cost in the consolidated financial statements                    之財務資產及財務負債的賬面值
                 approximate their fair values due to short-term                            與公平值相若。
                 maturities.

21. RETIREMENT BENEFITS SCHEME                                                       21. 退休福利計劃
     The Group operates a MPF scheme for all qualifying employees in                   本集團為所有合資格香港僱員經營強積金
     Hong Kong. Under the scheme, the employer and its employees                       計 劃。根 據 該 計 劃，僱 主 及 其 僱 員 須 分 別
     are each required to make contributions to the plan at 5% of the                  向該計劃作出僱員有關入息5%的供款，供
     employees’ relevant income, subject to a cap of monthly relevant                  款金額受每月有關入息上限30,000港元所
     income of HK$30,000. The assets of the schemes are held                           限。該計劃之資產與本集團之資產分開，並
     separately from those of the Group, in funds under the control                    以受託人管理之基金持有。本集團每月向
     of trustees. The Group contributes 5% of relevant payroll costs                   強積金計劃作出有關薪金成本5%的供款，
     capped at HK$1,500 per month to MPF scheme, in which the                          上限為1,500港元，其中供款與僱員匹配。
     contribution is matched by employees.

     contributions paid and payable to these plans by the Group at                     劃之規則所訂明之比率已付及應付該等計
